<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">for all</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x < x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">We take</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z ></span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 0 ﬁrst. As</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is strictly decreasing,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">is also strictly decreasing. Then,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is a</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">continuous and strictly increasing function. As</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> θ <</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 0, we also have</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> > x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">. Take arbitrary</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x, x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">, y</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> such that</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x < x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x < y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z ></span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 0. Then, we have</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">. Deﬁne</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> y,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">=</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">=</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">+</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">By the deﬁnition of</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">, we have</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">+</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">By the mean-value theorem, there exists</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ∈</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> (</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">y, y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">),</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">∈</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> (</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">, y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">+</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">),</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ∈</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> (</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x, x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) and</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">Furthermore,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x, x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">, y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">satisfy</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x < x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">< y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x < y < y</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">. If</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is small enough, then</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">will also be small enough as</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is continuous. Therefore, we have</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ξ < ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">< ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ξ < ζ < ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">when</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">is small enough.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">In Step 2, we have that log</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) is convex when 1</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">/η</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is convex. Therefore, we get</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">log(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)) + log(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ⩾</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> log(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)) + log(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">,</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">for all</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> a < a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">< b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> a < b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">< b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">, which means</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ⩾</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">As</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> g</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">), we have</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">1</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">⩾</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">1</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">a</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">b</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">))</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">As</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) is strictly decreasing, it means that</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">α</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">β</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ⩽</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">α</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">β</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">for</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> α > α</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">> β</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> α > β</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">> β</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">. Therefore, we have</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ⩽</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) as</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">> ζ > ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> and</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">> ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">> ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">. That is,</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">z</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">⩽</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ξ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">z</span>

<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">s</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">ζ</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">=</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">On the other hand,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> =</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) and</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> h</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">=</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">+</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">). Therefore,</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ⩽</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> </span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">+</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">when</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is small enough. If</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> z <</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 0, we can also get (</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">15</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) by an analogous argument.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">Hence, the second-order derivative of</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> G</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is increasing for each</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> θ <</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 0, which means that</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> �→</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">F</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">�</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(1</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">F</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">�</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">is convex for all</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ∈</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> (0</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 1) if 1</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">/η</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is convex.</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">An analogous argument yields that</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> �→</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> F</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">�</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(1</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> −</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">)</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">F</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">(</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">x</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">) +</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">�</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">is concave for all</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> p</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> ∈</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> (0</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">,</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> 1) when</span>
<span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">1</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;">/η</span><span  style="font-size: 9.962639808654785px;"> is concave.</span>
